The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n        # JADE adaptive memory and external archive\n        self.archive = []\n        self.archive_capacity = max(1, self.pop_size)\n        self.success_F = []\n        self.success_CR = []\n        self.memory_size = 10\n        self.memory_F = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.5)\n        self.memory_CR = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.9)\n        self.memory_idx = 0\n        self.p_best = 0.05\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # ---- JADE-style adaptive mutation / crossover with archive ----\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            trial_F = np.full(pop_size, F)\n            trial_CR = np.full(pop_size, CR)\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Build combined population + external archive\n            combined_pop = list(pop)\n            combined_fit = list(fitness)\n            if len(self.archive) > 0:\n                combined_pop.extend([ind for ind, _ in self.archive])\n                combined_fit.extend([f for _, f in self.archive])\n            combined_len = len(combined_pop)\n\n            # Random memory index for each individual\n            r_mem = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size, size=pop_size)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR\n                if len(self.success_F) > 0 or len(self.success_CR) > 0:\n                    # JADE: F from Cauchy(mu_F, 0.1), CR from Normal(mu_CR, 0.1)\n                    mu_F = float(self.memory_F[r_mem[i]])\n                    mu_CR = float(self.memory_CR[r_mem[i]])\n                    f_i = mu_F + 0.1 * float(rng.standard_cauchy())\n                    cr_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * float(rng.randn())\n                    f_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, f_i))\n                    cr_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, cr_i))\n                else:\n                    f_i = F\n                    cr_i = CR\n                trial_F[i] = f_i\n                trial_CR[i] = cr_i\n\n                # pbest selection: random among top p*100% population (excluding i itself)\n                p_num = max(1, int(self.p_best * pop_size))\n                top_indices = sorted_idx[:p_num]\n                valid_pbest = [idx for idx in top_indices if idx != i]\n                if len(valid_pbest) == 0:\n                    pbest_idx = i\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = valid_pbest[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_pbest)))]\n\n                # r1 from population, excluding i and pbest\n                pop_candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(pop_candidates) == 0:\n                    r1_idx = i\n                else:\n                    r1_idx = pop_candidates[int(rng.randint(0, len(pop_candidates)))]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1_idx]\n\n                # x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                xi = pop[i]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n                else:\n                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = mu_F\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = mu_CR\n                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n                self.success_F = []\n                self.success_CR = []\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\"\n    }\n    </json>",

x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                xi = pop[i]\n\n                # Record successful adaptive parameters\n                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n                else:\n                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = mu_F\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = mu_CR\n                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n                self.success_F = []\n                self.success_CR = []\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\"\n    }\n    </json>"

domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n        log_fn: logging function\n        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if problem from the suite\n    problem = next(iter(suite))\n\n    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    try:\n        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n        \n        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n            import torch\n            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n\n    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n    \n    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n    \n    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n    \n    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n    if mode == 'train':\n        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n        if training_loss is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n        if sweep_return is not None:\n            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n        return {\n            'score': score,\n            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'opt_val': opt_val,\n            'training_loss': training_loss,\n cr_start\n    91\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    92\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    93\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    94\t       self.last_gbest = None\n   100\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   101\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   102\t\n   103\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   104\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   105\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   106\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   107\t\n   108\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   109\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   110\t        CR = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   114\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   115\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   116\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   117\t            else:\n   118\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   119\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   120\t\n   121\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   122\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   123\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   124\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   125\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   126\t\n   127\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   128\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   129\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   130\t        else:\n   131\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   132\t\n   133\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   134\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   135\t        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\n   136\t        log_eps_end = self.epsilon_start))\n   145\t\n   146\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   147\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n\nif torch is not None:\n    class _Net(nn.Module):\n        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n            if seed is not None:\n                self._init_weights(seed)\n\n        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n            g = torch.Generator()\n            g.manual_seed(seed)\n            \n            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            self.load(model_path)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n        \n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n        else:\n            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n\n        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n        else:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n\n        if self.training:\n            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n            self._last_obs = observation\n            self._last_action_idx = idx\n            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n\n        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n\n    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n        self.total_reward += reward\n        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n        \n        if not self.training:\n---END---\n\"\"\"Environment wrapper to train DQN to control DE parameters.\n\nImplements a tiny OpenAI Gym-like interface: reset(problem, max_evals, init_x),\nstep(action) -> (obs, reward, done, info).\n\nReward convention: 1.0 if a strictly better solution is found during the\nstep, 0.0 otherwise.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Tuple, Optional\n\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\nthis_dir = os.path.dirname(__file__)\ndef _load_local(name, filename):\n    path = os.path.join(this_dir, filename)\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)\n    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(module)\n    return module\n\n# load local ec_algorithm module\n_ec_mod = _load_local('cocoex_example.ec_algorithm', 'ec_algorithm.py')\nDEOptimizer = _ec_mod.DEOptimizer\n\n\nclass DQNEnv:\n    def __init__(self, step_budget: int = 20, pop_size: int = None):\n        self.problem = None\n        self.max_evals = 0\n        self.step_budget = int(step_budget)\n        self.pop_size = pop_size\n        self.total_evals = 0\n        self.current_best_x = None\n        self.current_best_f = float('inf')\n        self.done = False\n\n    def reset(self, problem, max_evals: int, init_x: Optional[list] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Attach a cocoex `problem` and initialize internal state.\n\n        Returns initial observation.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.problem = problem\n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.total_evals = 0\n        self.done = False\n        if init_x is None:\n            try:\n                init_x = problem.initial_solution\n            except Exception:\n                init_x = [0] * problem.dimension\n        self.current_best_x = list(init_x)\n        f = float(problem(self.current_best_x))\n        self.current_best_f = f\n        self.init_best_f = f\n        self.total_evals = problem.evaluations\n        return self._get_obs()\n\n    def step(self, action: Any) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], float, bool, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Apply action (dict or array-like) as DE params, run DE for a\n        short `step_budget` evaluations starting from current best, and return\n        observation, reward, done, info.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.done:\n            return self._get_obs(), 0.0, True, {}\n\n        # normalize action into params dict\n        params = {}\n        if isinstance(action, dict):\n            params.update(action)\n        else:\n            # assume array-like [F, CR]\n            params['F'] = float(action[0])\n            params['CR'] = float(action[1])\n\n        # run a short DE search\n        de = DEOptimizer(self.problem.dimension, getattr(self.problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(self.problem, 'upper_bounds', None), self.step_budget, params=params, swarm_size=self.pop_size)\n        xopt, stats = de.optimize(self.problem, x0=self.current_best_x)\n\n        new_best = stats.get('gbest_f', float('inf'))\n        new_x = stats.get('gbest', None)\n\n        # reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n        reward = 0.0\n        if new_best < self.current_best_f:\n            reward = 1.0\n            self.current_best_f = new_best\n            if new_x is not None:\n                self.current_best_x = list(new_x)\n\n        # update eval counters\n        self.total_evals = stats.get('evals', self.total_evals)\n        done = (self.total_evals >= self.max_evals) or getattr(self.problem, 'final_target_hit', False)\n        self.done = done\n\n        obs = self._get_obs()\n        info = {'de_stats': stats}\n        return obs, float(reward), done, info\n\n    def _get_obs(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        return {'iteration': 0, 'evals': int(self.total_evals), 'gbest_f': float(self.current_best_f), 'initial_gbest_f': float(self.init_best_f), 'max_evals': int(self.max_evals)}\n\n    def render(self):\n        print('evals', self.total_evals, 'best_f', self.current_best_f)\"\n    }\n    </json>",

It can load/save a model\nand be used at inference time inside the DE optimizer.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\ntry:\n    import torch\n    import torch.nn as nn\n    import torch.nn.functional as F\nexcept Exception:\n    torch = None\n\nfrom param_controller import ParamController\n\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED, set_global_seed\n\nif torch is not None:\n    class _Net(nn.Module):\n        def __init__(self, obs_dim, action_dim, seed=None):\n            super().__init__()\n            self.fc1 = nn.Linear(obs_dim, 128)\n            self.fc2 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.fc3 = nn.Linear(128, 128)\n            self.out = nn.Linear(128, action_dim)\n            \n            # \u5982\u679c\u63d0\u4f9b\u4e86\u79cd\u5b50\uff0c\u4f7f\u7528\u5b83\u6765\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u6743\u91cd\n            if seed is not None:\n                self._init_weights(seed)\n\n        def _init_weights(self, seed):\n            \"\"\"\u4f7f\u7528\u56fa\u5b9a\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\u6743\u91cd\"\"\"\n            g = torch.Generator()\n            g.manual_seed(seed)\n            \n            # \u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n\n        def forward(self, x):\n            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n            return self.out(x)\n\n\nclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n\n    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            self.load(model_path)\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n        \n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n        \n        with torch.no_grad():\n            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n            try:\n                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n            except Exception:\n                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n\n        out = out_t.numpy()\n\n        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n        try:\n            f_raw = float(out[0])\n            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            f_sig = 0.5\n        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n        \n        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n        try:\n            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n        except Exception:\n            cr_sig = 0.9\n        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n        \n        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n\n    def save(self, path: str):\n        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n\n    def load(self, path: str):\n        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\n\nclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n\n    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n    The network outputs Q-values for each discrete action; `step`\n    returns the greedy action as a dict (matching ParamController).\n\n    Online training: stores transitions in a replay buffer and performs\n    a DQN update every `update_freq` steps.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, bins: int = 5,\n                device: str = 'cpu', training: bool = False, update_freq: int = 10,\n                buffer_capacity: int = 1000, batch_size: int = 64,\n                lr: float = 1e-4, gamma: float = 0.99, target_update_freq: int = 50,\n                max_grad_norm: float = 1.0, epsilon_start: float = 1.0,\n                epsilon_end: float = 0.05, epsilon_decay: float = 0.999,\n                seed: int = None):\n        if torch is None:\n            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DiscreteDQNController')\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n        self.bins = int(bins)\n        self.seed = seed if seed is not None else 42\n        \n        # \u8bbe\u7f6ePyTorch\u79cd\u5b50\u786e\u4fdd\u7f51\u7edc\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u53ef\u590d\u73b0\n        torch.manual_seed(self.seed)\n        if torch.cuda.is_available():\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed(self.seed)\n            torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(self.seed)\n        \n        # \u786e\u4fddPyTorch\u64cd\u4f5c\u5b8c\u5168\u786e\u5b9a\u6027\n        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True\n        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False\n        torch.backends.cudnn.enabled = False  # \u7981\u7528cudnn\u4ee5\u5b8c\u5168\u6d88\u9664\u968f\u673a\u6027\n        \n        self.rng = np.random.RandomState(self.seed)\n\n        # DE parameter discretization\n        self.F_vals = np.linspace(0.1, 1.0, self.bins)\n        self.CR_vals = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, self.bins)\n\n        self.actions = []\n        for fi in self.F_vals:\n            for cri in self.CR_vals:\n                self.actions.append({'F': float(fi), 'CR': float(cri)})\n        self.n_actions = len(self.actions)\n        \n        # \u4f7f\u7528\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u7f51\u7edc\n        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n\n        # Training setup\n        self.training = training\n        self.update_freq = update_freq\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\n        self.gamma = gamma\n        self.target_update_freq = target_update_freq\n        self.step_count = 0\n        self.total_loss = 0.0\n        self.total_reward = 0.0\n        self.loss_history = []\n        self.reward_history = []\n        self.return_record_interval = 5\n        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n\n        self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm\n        self.epsilon = epsilon_start\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n        self.epsilon_decay = epsilon_decay\n        if training:\n            # target_net\u4e5f\u9700\u8981\u4f7f\u7528\u76f8\u540c\u79cd\u5b50\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n            self.target_net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.n_actions, seed=self.seed).to(self.device)\n            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n            self.optimizer = torch.optim.Adam(self.net.parameters(), lr=lr)\n            self.buffer_capacity = buffer_capacity\n            self.replay_buffer = []\n            self.buffer_pos = 0\n\n        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n            try:\n                self.load(model_path)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n        return None\n\n    def _obs_to_tensor(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> torch.Tensor:\n        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n        else:\n            rel_improve = 0.0\n        # Normalize gbest_f to [-1, 1] range using tanh of log scale to avoid explosion\n        if np.isfinite(gbest_f) and gbest_f != 0.0:\n            gbest_f_norm = float(np.tanh(np.log10(abs(gbest_f)) * np.sign(gbest_f) / 10.0))\n        else:\n            gbest_f_norm = 0.0\n        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f_norm]\n        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n\n        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n        else:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n\n        if self.training:\n            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n            self._last_obs = observation\n            self._last_action_idx = idx\n            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n\n        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n\n    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n        self.total_reward += reward\n        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n        \n        if not self.training:\n---END---\n\"\"\"Environment wrapper to train DQN to control DE parameters.\n\nImplements a tiny OpenAI Gym-like interface: reset(problem, max_evals, init_x),\nstep(action) -> (obs, reward, done, info).\n\nReward convention: 1.0 if a strictly better solution is found during the\nstep, 0.0 otherwise.\n\"\"\"\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Tuple, Optional\n\nimport os\nimport importlib.util\n\n# Import global configuration\nimport sys\nimport os\ntry:\n    sys.path.insert(0, '/hada')\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\n    set_global_seed()\nexcept Exception:\n    SEED = 42\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\n        pass\n\nthis_dir = os.path.dirname(__file__)\ndef _load_local(name, filename):\n    path = os.path.join(this_dir, filename)\n    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)\n    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    spec.loader.exec_module(module)\n    return module\n\n# load local ec_algorithm module\n_ec_mod = _load_local('cocoex_example.ec_algorithm', 'ec_algorithm.py')\nDEOptimizer = _ec_mod.DEOptimizer\n\n\nclass DQNEnv:\n    def __init__(self, step_budget: int = 20, pop_size: int = None):\n        self.problem = None\n        self.max_evals = 0\n        self.step_budget = int(step_budget)\n        self.pop_size = pop_size\n        self.total_evals = 0\n        self.current_best_x = None\n        self.current_best_f = float('inf')\n        self.done = False\n\n    def reset(self, problem, max_evals: int, init_x: Optional[list] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Attach a cocoex `problem` and initialize internal state.\n\n        Returns initial observation.\n        \"\"\"\n        self.problem = problem\n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.total_evals = 0\n        self.done = False\n        if init_x is None:\n            try:\n                init_x = problem.initial_solution\n            except Exception:\n                init_x = [0] * problem.dimension\n        self.current_best_x = list(init_x)\n        f = float(problem(self.current_best_x))\n        self.current_best_f = f\n        self.init_best_f = f\n        self.total_evals = problem.evaluations\n        return self._get_obs()\n\n    def step(self, action: Any) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], float, bool, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Apply action (dict or array-like) as DE params, run DE for a\n        short `step_budget` evaluations starting from current best, and return\n        observation, reward, done, info.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.done:\n            return self._get_obs(), 0.0, True, {}\n\n        # normalize action into params dict\n        params = {}\n        if isinstance(action, dict):\n            params.update(action)\n        else:\n            # assume array-like [F, CR]\n            params['F'] = float(action[0])\n            params['CR'] = float(action[1])\n\n        # run a short DE search\n        de = DEOptimizer(self.problem.dimension, getattr(self.problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(self.problem, 'upper_bounds', None), self.step_budget, params=params, swarm_size=self.pop_size)\n        xopt, stats = de.optimize(self.problem, x0=self.current_best_x)\n\n        new_best = stats.get('gbest_f', float('inf'))\n        new_x = stats.get('gbest', None)\n\n        # reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n        reward = 0.0\n        if new_best < self.current_best_f:\n            reward = 1.0\n            self.current_best_f = new_best\n            if new_x is not None:\n                self.current_best_x = list(new_x)\n\n        # update eval counters\n        self.total_evals = stats.get('evals', self.total_evals)\n        done = (self.total_evals >= self.max_evals) or getattr(self.problem, 'final_target_hit', False)\n        self.done = done\n\n        obs = self._get_obs()\n        info = {'de_stats': stats}\n        return obs, float(reward), done, info\n\n    def _get_obs(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        return {'iteration': 0, 'evals': int(self.total_evals), 'gbest_f': float(self.current_best_f), 'initial_gbest_f': float(self.init_best_f), 'max_evals': int(self.max_evals)}\n\n    def render(self):\n        print('evals', self.total_evals, 'best_f', self.current_best_f)\"\n    }\n    </json>"

x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n   196\t                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n   197\t                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n   198\t                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n   199\t                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n   200\t                else:\n   201\t                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n   202\t                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n   203\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n   204\t                else:\n   205\t                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n   206\t\n   207\t                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n   208\t                xi = pop[i]\n   209\t\n   210\t                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   211\t                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n   212\t\n   213\t                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n   214\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n   215\t                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n   216\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   217\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n   218\t\n   219\t                # Boundary handling: clip\n   220\t                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n   221\t\n   222\t            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n   223\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   224\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   225\t                    done = True\n   226\t                    break\n   227\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   228\t                evals += 1\n   229\t\n   230\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   231\t                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n   232\t                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n   233\t                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n   234\t                    else:\n   235\t                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n   236\t                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n   237\t\n   238\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   239\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   240\t                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n   241\t                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n   242\t                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n   243\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   244\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   245\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   246\t\n   247\t            # Update JADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n   248\t            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n   249\t                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n   250\t                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n   251\t                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n   252\t                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n   253\t                else:\n   254\t                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n   255\t                mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n   256\t                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = mu_F\n   257\t                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = mu_CR\n   258\t                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n   259\t                self.success_F = []\n   260\t                self.success_CR = []\n   261\t            \n   262\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   263\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   264\t            \n   265\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   266\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   267\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   268\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   269\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   270\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   271\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   272\t            \n   273\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   274\t\n   275\t        self.stats = {\n   276\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   277\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   278\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   279\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   280\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   281\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   282\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   283\t        }\n   284\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'cat -n /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py'},\n        \"tool_output\": \"1\t\"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer for cocoex problems.\n     2\t\n     3\tThis module provides a `DEOptimizer` class that is controlled by a DQN agent\n     4\tfor hyperparameter optimization. The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\n     5\tobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\n     6\tadjustment (F, CR).\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\t\n     9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = None\n    16\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    17\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    18\t\n    19\t\n    20\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n    42\t                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n    43\t                 swarm_size: int = None):\n    44\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t        # JADE adaptive memory and external archive\n    69\t        self.archive = []\n    70\t        self.archive_capacity = max(1, self.pop_size)\n    71\t        self.success_F = []\n    72\t        self.success_CR = []\n    73\t        self.memory_size = 10\n    74\t        self.memory_F = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.5)\n    75\t        self.memory_CR = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.9)\n    76\t        self.memory_idx = 0\n    77\t        self.p_best = 0.05\n    78\t\n    79\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    80\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    81\t        selected = []\n    82\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    83\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    84\t            selected.append(idx)\n    85\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    86\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    87\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    88\t                best_idx = idx\n    89\t        return best_idx\n    90\t\n    91\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    92\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    93\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n    94\t\n    95\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    96\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    97\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    98\t        \n    99\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n   100\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n   101\t\n   102\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n   103\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   104\t        \n   105\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   106\t        evals = 0\n   107\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   108\t        \n   109\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   110\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   111\t                break\n   112\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   113\t            evals += 1\n   114\t\n   115\t        # Track best\n   116\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   117\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   118\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   119\t        \n   120\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   121\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   122\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   123\t\n   124\t        # Main loop\n   125\t        iteration = 0\n   126\t        done = False\n   127\t        \n   128\t        while not done:\n   129\t            iteration += 1\n   130\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   131\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   132\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   133\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   134\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   135\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   136\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   137\t            \n   138\t            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   139\t            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   140\t            \n   141\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   142\t            \n   143\t            # ---- JADE-style adaptive mutation / crossover with archive ----\n   144\t            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n   145\t            trial_F = np.full(pop_size, F)\n   146\t            trial_CR = np.full(pop_size, CR)\n   147\t\n   148\t            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\n   149\t            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n   150\t\n   151\t            # Build combined population + external archive\n   152\t            combined_pop = list(pop)\n   153\t            combined_fit = list(fitness)\n   154\t            if len(self.archive) > 0:\n   155\t                combined_pop.extend([ind for ind, _ in self.archive])\n   156\t                combined_fit.extend([f for _, f in self.archive])\n   157\t            combined_len = len(combined_pop)\n   158\t\n   159\t            # Random memory index for each individual\n   160\t            r_mem = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size, size=pop_size)\n   161\t\n   162\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   163\t                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR\n   164\t                if len(self.success_F) > 0 or len(self.success_CR) > 0:\n   165\t                    # JADE: F from Cauchy(mu_F, 0.1), CR from Normal(mu_CR, 0.1)\n   166\t                    mu_F = float(self.memory_F[r_mem[i]])\n   167\t                    mu_CR = float(self.memory_CR[r_mem[i]])\n   168\t                    f_i = mu_F + 0.1 * float(rng.standard_cauchy())\n   169\t                    cr_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * float(rng.randn())\n   170\t                    f_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, f_i))\n   171\t                    cr_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, cr_i))\n   172\t                else:\n   173\t                    f_i = F\n   174\t                    cr_i = CR\n   175\t                trial_F[i] = f_i\n   176\t                trial_CR[i] = cr_i\n   177\t\n   178\t                # pbest selection: random among top p*100% population (excluding i itself)\n   179\t                p_num = max(1, int(self.p_best * pop_size))\n   180\t                top_indices = sorted_idx[:p_num]\n   181\t                valid_pbest = [idx for idx in top_indices if idx != i]\n   182\t                if len(valid_pbest) == 0:\n   183\t                    pbest_idx = i\n   184\t                else:\n   185\t                    pbest_idx = valid_pbest[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_pbest)))]\n   186\t\n   187\t                # r1 from population, excluding i and pbest\n   188\t                pop_candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n   189\t                if len(pop_candidates) == 0:\n   190\t                    r1_idx = i\n   191\t                else:\n   192\t                    r1_idx = pop_candidates[int(rng.randint(0, len(pop_candidates)))]\n   193\t                x_r1 = pop[r1_idx]\n   194\t\n   195\t                # x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n   196\t                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n   197\t                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n   198\t                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n   199\t                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n   200\t                else:\n   201\t                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n   202\t                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n   203\t                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n   204\t                else:\n   205\t                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n   206\t\n   207\t                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n   208\t                xi = pop[i]\n   209\t\n   210\t                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   211\t                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n   212\t\n   213\t                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n   214\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n   215\t                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n   216\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   217\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n   218\t\n   219\t                # Boundary handling: clip\n   220\t                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n   221\t\n   222\t            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n   223\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   224\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   225\t                    done = True\n   226\t                    break\n   227\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   228\t                evals += 1\n   229\t\n   230\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   231\t                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n   232\t                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n   233\t                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n   234\t                    else:\n   235\t                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n   236\t                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n   237\t\n   238\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   239\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   240\t                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n   241\t                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n   242\t                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n   243\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   244\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   245\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   246\t\n   247\t            # Update JADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n   248\t            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n   249\t                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n   250\t                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n   251\t                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n   252\t                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n   253\t                else:\n   254\t                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n   255\t                mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n   256\t                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = mu_F\n   257\t                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = mu_CR\n   258\t                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n   259\t                self.success_F = []\n   260\t                self.success_CR = []\n   261\t            \n   262\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   263\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   264\t            \n   265\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   266\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   267\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   268\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   269\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   270\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   271\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   272\t            \n   273\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   274\t\n   275\t        self.stats = {\n   276\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   277\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   278\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   279\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   280\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   281\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   282\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   283\t        }\n   284\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>"
