Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '1,360p' /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"\"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer for cocoex problems.\n\nThis module provides a `DEOptimizer` class that is controlled by a DQN agent\nfor hyperparameter optimization. The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n        # JADE/SHADE adaptive memory and external archive\n        self.archive = []\n        self.archive_capacity = max(1, self.pop_size)\n        self.success_F = []\n        self.success_CR = []\n        self.memory_size = 10\n        self.memory_F = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.5)\n        self.memory_CR = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.9)\n        self.memory_idx = 0\n        self.p_best = self.params.get('p_best', 0.1)\n        self.memory_c = self.params.get('memory_c', 0.1)\n        self.local_search_budget_ratio = float(self.params.get('local_search_budget_ratio', 0.15))\n        self.stagnation_limit = int(self.params.get('stagnation_limit', 8))\n        self.restart_radius_factor = float(self.params.get('restart_radius_factor', 0.2))\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # ---- JADE-style adaptive mutation / crossover with archive ----\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            trial_F = np.full(pop_size, F)\n            trial_CR = np.full(pop_size, CR)\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Build combined population + external archive\n            combined_pop = list(pop)\n            combined_fit = list(fitness)\n            if len(self.archive) > 0:\n                combined_pop.extend([ind for ind, _ in self.archive])\n                combined_fit.extend([f for _, f in self.archive])\n            combined_len = len(combined_pop)\n\n            # Random memory index for each individual\n            r_mem = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size, size=pop_size)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR\n                if len(self.success_F) > 0 or len(self.success_CR) > 0:\n                    # JADE: F from Cauchy(mu_F, 0.1), CR from Normal(mu_CR, 0.1)\n                    mu_F = float(self.memory_F[r_mem[i]])\n                    mu_CR = float(self.memory_CR[r_mem[i]])\n                    f_i = mu_F + 0.1 * float(rng.standard_cauchy())\n                    cr_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * float(rng.randn())\n                    f_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, f_i))\n                    cr_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, cr_i))\n                else:\n                    f_i = F\n                    cr_i = CR\n                trial_F[i] = f_i\n                trial_CR[i] = cr_i\n\n                # pbest selection: random among top p*100% population (excluding i itself)\n                p_num = max(1, int(self.p_best * pop_size))\n                top_indices = sorted_idx[:p_num]\n                valid_pbest = [idx for idx in top_indices if idx != i]\n                if len(valid_pbest) == 0:\n                    pbest_idx = i\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = valid_pbest[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_pbest)))]\n\n                # r1 from population, excluding i and pbest\n                pop_candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(pop_candidates) == 0:\n                    r1_idx = i\n                else:\n                    r1_idx = pop_candidates[int(rng.randint(0, len(pop_candidates)))]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1_idx]\n\n                # x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                xi = pop[i]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n                else:\n                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = mu_F\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = mu_CR\n                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n                self.success_F = []\n                self.success_CR = []\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '1,360p' /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"\"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer for cocoex problems.\n\nThis module provides a `DEOptimizer` class that is controlled by a DQN agent\nfor hyperparameter optimization. The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n        # JADE/SHADE adaptive memory and external archive\n        self.archive = []\n        self.archive_capacity = max(1, self.pop_size)\n        self.success_F = []\n        self.success_CR = []\n        self.memory_size = 10\n        self.memory_F = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.5)\n        self.memory_CR = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.9)\n        self.memory_idx = 0\n        self.p_best = self.params.get('p_best', 0.1)\n        self.memory_c = self.params.get('memory_c', 0.1)\n        self.local_search_budget_ratio = float(self.params.get('local_search_budget_ratio', 0.15))\n        self.stagnation_limit = int(self.params.get('stagnation_limit', 8))\n        self.restart_radius_factor = float(self.params.get('restart_radius_factor', 0.2))\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # ---- JADE-style adaptive mutation / crossover with archive ----\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            trial_F = np.full(pop_size, F)\n            trial_CR = np.full(pop_size, CR)\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Build combined population + external archive\n            combined_pop = list(pop)\n            combined_fit = list(fitness)\n            if len(self.archive) > 0:\n                combined_pop.extend([ind for ind, _ in self.archive])\n                combined_fit.extend([f for _, f in self.archive])\n            combined_len = len(combined_pop)\n\n            # Random memory index for each individual\n            r_mem = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size, size=pop_size)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR\n                if len(self.success_F) > 0 or len(self.success_CR) > 0:\n                    # JADE: F from Cauchy(mu_F, 0.1), CR from Normal(mu_CR, 0.1)\n                    mu_F = float(self.memory_F[r_mem[i]])\n                    mu_CR = float(self.memory_CR[r_mem[i]])\n                    f_i = mu_F + 0.1 * float(rng.standard_cauchy())\n                    cr_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * float(rng.randn())\n                    f_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, f_i))\n                    cr_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, cr_i))\n                else:\n                    f_i = F\n                    cr_i = CR\n                trial_F[i] = f_i\n                trial_CR[i] = cr_i\n\n                # pbest selection: random among top p*100% population (excluding i itself)\n                p_num = max(1, int(self.p_best * pop_size))\n                top_indices = sorted_idx[:p_num]\n                valid_pbest = [idx for idx in top_indices if idx != i]\n                if len(valid_pbest) == 0:\n                    pbest_idx = i\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = valid_pbest[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_pbest)))]\n\n                # r1 from population, excluding i and pbest\n                pop_candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(pop_candidates) == 0:\n                    r1_idx = i\n                else:\n                    r1_idx = pop_candidates[int(rng.randint(0, len(pop_candidates)))]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1_idx]\n\n                # x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                xi = pop[i]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n                else:\n                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = mu_F\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = mu_CR\n                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n                self.success_F = []\n                self.success_CR = []\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>"

lb).max() if np.isscalar(ub) else 0.02 * float(np.max(ub - lb))\\n        while evals < self.max_evals and budget > 0:\\n            improved = False\\n            order = rng.permutation(self.dim)\\n            for j in order:\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals or budget <= 0:\\n                    break\\n                old_x = float(gbest[j])\\n                for direction in (1.0, -1.0):\\n                    cand = gbest.copy()\\n                    cand[j] = old_x + direction * step\\n                    cand = np.clip(cand, lb, ub)\\n                    val = evaluate_problem(problem, cand)\\n                    evals += 1\\n                    budget -= 1\\n                    if budget <= 0:\\n                        break\\n                    if val < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest = cand\\n                        gbest_f = val\\n                        improved = True\\n                        break\\n                if budget <= 0:\\n                    break\\n            if not improved:\\n                step *= 0.5\\n                if step < 1e-12 * (float(np.max(ub - lb)) if not np.isscalar(ub) else 1.0):\\n                    break\\n            if budget <= 0:\\n                break\\n        return evals, gbest, gbest_f\\n\\n    def _restart_population(self, pop, fitness, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, radius_factor):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Restart a fraction of the population around the current best to escape stagnation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        n_replace = max(1, int(0.5 * len(pop)))\\n        radius = radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb)) if np.isscalar(ub) else radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb))\\n        for i in range(n_replace):\\n            pop[i] = np.clip(gbest + rng.normal(0.0, radius, self.dim), lb, ub)\\n            # keep their fitness stale; will be re-evaluated lazily as inf\\n            fitness[i] = np.inf\\n        # Also remove archive entries to allow fresh diversity\\n        if len(self.archive) > 0:\\n            keep = max(1, len(self.archive) // 2)\\n            self.archive = self.archive[:keep]\\n        return pop, fitness\\n\\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",

Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '130,340p' /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"return evals, gbest, gbest_f\n\n    def _restart_population(self, pop, fitness, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, radius_factor):\n        \"\"\"Restart a fraction of the population around the current best to escape stagnation.\"\"\"\n        n_replace = max(1, int(0.5 * len(pop)))\n        radius = radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb)) if np.isscalar(ub) else radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb))\n        for i in range(n_replace):\n            pop[i] = np.clip(gbest + rng.normal(0.0, radius, self.dim), lb, ub)\n            # keep their fitness stale; will be re-evaluated lazily as inf\n            fitness[i] = np.inf\n        # Also remove archive entries to allow fresh diversity\n        if len(self.archive) > 0:\n            keep = max(1, len(self.archive) // 2)\n            self.archive = self.archive[:keep]\n        return pop, fitness\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # ---- JADE-style adaptive mutation / crossover with archive ----\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            trial_F = np.full(pop_size, F)\n            trial_CR = np.full(pop_size, CR)\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Build combined population + external archive\n            combined_pop = list(pop)\n            combined_fit = list(fitness)\n            if len(self.archive) > 0:\n                combined_pop.extend([ind for ind, _ in self.archive])\n                combined_fit.extend([f for _, f in self.archive])\n            combined_len = len(combined_pop)\n\n            # Random memory index for each individual\n            r_mem = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size, size=pop_size)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR\n                if len(self.success_F) > 0 or len(self.success_CR) > 0:\n                    # JADE: F from Cauchy(mu_F, 0.1), CR from Normal(mu_CR, 0.1)\n                    mu_F = float(self.memory_F[r_mem[i]])\n                    mu_CR = float(self.memory_CR[r_mem[i]])\n                    f_i = mu_F + 0.1 * float(rng.standard_cauchy())\n                    cr_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * float(rng.randn())\n                    f_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, f_i))\n                    cr_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, cr_i))\n                else:\n                    f_i = F\n                    cr_i = CR\n                trial_F[i] = f_i\n                trial_CR[i] = cr_i\n\n                # pbest selection: random among top p*100% population (excluding i itself)\n                p_num = max(1, int(self.p_best * pop_size))\n                top_indices = sorted_idx[:p_num]\n                valid_pbest = [idx for idx in top_indices if idx != i]\n                if len(valid_pbest) == 0:\n                    pbest_idx = i\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = valid_pbest[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_pbest)))]\n\n                # r1 from population, excluding i and pbest\n                pop_candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(pop_candidates) == 0:\n                    r1_idx = i\n                else:\n                    r1_idx = pop_candidates[int(rng.randint(0, len(pop_candidates)))]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1_idx]\n\n                # x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                xi = pop[i]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n                else:\n                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n                mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = mu_F\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = mu_CR\n                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n                self.success_F = []\n                self.success_CR = []\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\\n\\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\\n\\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\\n        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - t) + self.f_end * t\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\\n\\n        # Blend in SHADE-style memory if it has meaningful content\\n        mem_F = float(np.mean(self.memory_F))\\n        mem_CR = float(np.mean(self.memory_CR))\\n        w = 0.25 * t  # rely more on memory later in the run\\n        F = (1.0 - Detects stagnation and boosts diversity / requests population restarts.\\n    - Exposes JADE/SHADE hyper-parameters (p_best, memory_c, restart radius,\\n      local search budget) so the optimizer can use them without code changes.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\\n                 stagnation_boost=0.2, memory_size=10, memory_c=0.1,\\n                 p_best=0.1, stagnation_limit=8, restart_radius_factor=0.2,\\n                 local_search_budget_ratio=0.15):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.memory_size = memory_size\\n        self.memory_c = memory_c\\n        self.memory_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        self.memory_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.9)\\n        self.memory_idx = 0\\n        self.p_best = p_best\\n        self.stagnation_limit = stagnation_limit\\n        self.restart_radius_factor = restart_radius_factor\\n        self.local_search_budget_ratio = local_search_budget_ratio\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get(\\'iteration\\', self.it + 1)\\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get(\\'max_evals\\', 10000) / 100))\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\\n\\n        # Progress-based exploration -> exploitation baseline\\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\\n      use \\'rand/1\\' strategy to explore broadly.\\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\\n      good building blocks), switch to \\'jade\\' for fast convergence.\\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get(\\'iteration\\', self.it + 1)\\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get(\\'max_evals\\', 10000) / 100))\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\\n\\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\\n\\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\\n        current_gbest = observation.get(\\'gbest_f\\', None)\\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\\n\\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\\n            strategy = \\'rand/1\\'\\n        else:\\n            strategy = \\'jade\\'\\n\\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\\n        log_eps_start = float(np.log(self.epsilon_start))\\n        log_eps_end = float(np.log(self.epsilon_end))\\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - \"\"\"Coordinate-wise local search around the best solution with adaptive step.\"\"\"\n        if budget <= 0:\n            return evals, gbest, gbest_f\n        step = 0.02 * (ub - lb).max() if np.isscalar(ub) else 0.02 * float(np.max(ub - lb))\n        while evals < self.max_evals and budget > 0:\n            improved = False\n            order = rng.permutation(self.dim)\n            for j in order:\n                if evals >= self.max_evals or budget <= 0:\n                    break\n                old_x = float(gbest[j])\n                for direction in (1.0, -1.0):\n                    cand = gbest.copy()\n                    cand[j] = old_x + direction * step\n                    cand = np.clip(cand, lb, ub)\n                    val = evaluate_problem(problem, cand)\n                    evals += 1\n                    budget -= 1\n                    if budget <= 0:\n                        break\n                    if val < gbest_f:\n                        gbest = cand\n                        gbest_f = val\n                        improved = True\n                        break\n                if budget <= 0:\n                    break\n            if not improved:\n                step *= 0.5\n                if step < 1e-12 * (float(np.max(ub - lb)) if not np.isscalar(ub) else 1.0):\n                    break\n            if budget <= 0:\n                break\n        return evals, gbest, gbest_f\n\n    def _restart_population(self, pop, fitness, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, radius_factor):\n        \"\"\"Restart a fraction of the population around the current best to escape stagnation.\"\"\"\n        n_replace = max(1, int(0.5 * len(pop)))\n        radius = radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb)) if np.isscalar(ub) else radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb))\n        for i in range(n_replace):\n            pop[i] = np.clip(gbest + rng.normal(0.0, radius, self.dim), lb, ub)\n            # keep their fitness stale; will be re-evaluated lazily as inf\n            fitness[i] = np.inf\n        # Also remove archive entries to allow fresh diversity\n        if len(self.archive) > 0:\n            keep = max(1, len(self.archive) // 2)\n            self.archive = self.archive[:keep]\n        return pop, fitness\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        # Reset per-run adaptive state (allows same optimizer object to be reused)\n        self.archive = []\n        self.success_F = []\n        self.success_CR = []\n        self.memory_idx = 0\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n\n        # If a known starting point is provided, use it as the first individual\n        if x0 is not None:\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).reshape(-1, self.dim)\n            pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr[0], lb, ub)\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n\n            # Allow the controller to dynamically tune JADE/SHADE hyper-parameters\n            self.p_best = float(params.get('p_best', self.p_best))\n            self.memory_c = float(params.get('memory_c', self.memory_c))\n            self.stagnation_limit = int(params.get('stagnation_limit', self.stagnation_limit))\n            self.restart_radius_factor = float(params.get('restart_radius_factor', self.restart_radius_factor))\n            self.local_search_budget_ratio = float(params.get('local_search_budget_ratio', self.local_search_budget_ratio))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # ---- JADE-style adaptive mutation / crossover with archive ----\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            trial_F = np.full(pop_size, F)\n            trial_CR = np.full(pop_size, CR)\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Build combined population + external archive\n            combined_pop = list(pop)\n            combined_fit = list(fitness)\n            if len(self.archive) > 0:\n                combined_pop.extend([ind for ind, _ in self.archive])\n                combined_fit.extend([f for _, f in self.archive])\n            combined_len = len(combined_pop)\n\n            # Random memory index for each individual\n            r_mem = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size, size=pop_size)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR\n                if len(self.success_F) > 0 or len(self.success_CR) > 0:\n                    # JADE: F from Cauchy(mu_F, 0.1), CR from Normal(mu_CR, 0.1)\n                    mu_F = float(self.memory_F[r_mem[i]])\n                    mu_CR = float(self.memory_CR[r_mem[i]])\n                    f_i = mu_F + 0.1 * float(rng.standard_cauchy())\n                    cr_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * float(rng.randn())\n                    f_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, f_i))\n                    cr_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, cr_i))\n                else:\n                    f_i = F\n                    cr_i = CR\n                trial_F[i] = f_i\n                trial_CR[i] = cr_i\n\n                # pbest selection: random among top p*100% population (excluding i itself)\n                p_num = max(1, int(self.p_best * pop_size))\n                top_indices = sorted_idx[:p_num]\n                valid_pbest = [idx for idx in top_indices if idx != i]\n                if len(valid_pbest) == 0:\n                    pbest_idx = i\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = valid_pbest[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_pbest)))]\n\n                # r1 from population, excluding i and pbest\n                pop_candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(pop_candidates) == 0:\n                    r1_idx = i\n                else:\n                    r1_idx = pop_candidates[int(rng.randint(0, len(pop_candidates)))]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1_idx]\n\n                # x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                xi = pop[i]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE/SHADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n                else:\n                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n                if len(succ_CR) > 0:\n                    mu_CR = float(np.sum(succ_CR * succ_CR) / np.sum(succ_CR)) if np.sum(succ_CR) > 1e-12 else float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n                else:\n                    mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR)) if len(succ_CR) > 0 else self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx]\n                # SHADE sliding memory update\n                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = (1.0 - self.memory_c) * self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] + self.memory_c * mu_F\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = (1.0 - self.memory_c) * self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] + self.memory_c * mu_CR\n                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n                self.success_F = []\n                self.success_CR = []\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Stagnation handling: restart half the population around best\n            if improved > 0.0:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n                if self.stagnation_count >= self.stagnation_limit:\n                    pop, _fitness_ignored = self._restart_population(pop, fitness, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, self.restart_radius_factor)\n                    self.stagnation_count = 0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        # Final local polish on the best solution using a small portion of remaining budget\n        remaining = self.max_evals - evals\n        if remaining > 0:\n            polish_budget = int(remaining * self.local_search_budget_ratio)\n            if polish_budget > 0:\n                evals, gbest, gbest_f = self._local_polish(problem, evals, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, polish_budget)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\nobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\nadjustment (F, CR).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Import SEED from config if available\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    \n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    \n    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n                 swarm_size: int = None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        if lower_bounds is not None:\n            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        if upper_bounds is not None:\n            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n        else:\n            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n        \n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n        # JADE/SHADE adaptive memory and external archive\n        self.archive = []\n        self.archive_capacity = max(1, self.pop_size)\n        self.success_F = []\n        self.success_CR = []\n        self.memory_size = 10\n        self.memory_F = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.5)\n        self.memory_CR = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.9)\n        self.memory_idx = 0\n        self.p_best = self.params.get('p_best', 0.1)\n        self.memory_c = self.params.get('memory_c', 0.1)\n        self.local_search_budget_ratio = float(self.params.get('local_search_budget_ratio', 0.15))\n        self.stagnation_limit = int(self.params.get('stagnation_limit', 8))\n        self.restart_radius_factor = float(self.params.get('restart_radius_factor', 0.2))\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n\n    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n        selected = []\n        for _ in range(tournsize):\n            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n            selected.append(idx)\n        best_idx = selected[0]\n        for idx in selected[1:]:\n            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n                best_idx = idx\n        return best_idx\n\n    def _local_polish(self, problem, evals, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, budget):\n        \"\"\"Coordinate-wise local search around the best solution with adaptive step.\"\"\"\n        if budget <= 0:\n            return evals, gbest, gbest_f\n        step = 0.02 * (ub - lb).max() if np.isscalar(ub) else 0.02 * float(np.max(ub - lb))\n        while evals < self.max_evals and budget > 0:\n            improved = False\n            order = rng.permutation(self.dim)\n            for j in order:\n                if evals >= self.max_evals or budget <= 0:\n                    break\n                old_x = float(gbest[j])\n                for direction in (1.0, -1.0):\n                    cand = gbest.copy()\n                    cand[j] = old_x + direction * step\n                    cand = np.clip(cand, lb, ub)\n                    val = evaluate_problem(problem, cand)\n                    evals += 1\n                    budget -= 1\n                    if val < gbest_f:\n                        gbest = cand\n                        gbest_f = val\n                        improved = True\n                    if budget <= 0:\n                        break\n                    if improved:\n                        break\n                if budget <= 0:\n                    break\n            if not improved:\n                step *= 0.5\n                if step < 1e-12 * (float(np.max(ub - lb)) if not np.isscalar(ub) else 1.0):\n                    break\n            if budget <= 0:\n                break\n        return evals, gbest, gbest_f\n\n    def _restart_population(self, pop, fitness, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, radius_factor):\n        \"\"\"Restart a fraction of the population around the current best to escape stagnation.\"\"\"\n        n_replace = max(1, int(0.5 * len(pop)))\n        radius = radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb)) if np.isscalar(ub) else radius_factor * float(np.max(ub - lb))\n        for i in range(n_replace):\n            pop[i] = np.clip(gbest + rng.normal(0.0, radius, self.dim), lb, ub)\n            # Assign a very large finite fitness so bad restarted individuals are\n            # not used as pbest while still being easy to replace after evaluation.\n            fitness[i] = 1e100\n        # Also remove archive entries to allow fresh diversity\n        if len(self.archive) > 0:\n            keep = max(1, len(self.archive) // 2)\n            self.archive = self.archive[:keep]\n        return pop, fitness\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n        if self.controller is not None:\n            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n\n        # Reset per-run adaptive state (allows same optimizer object to be reused)\n        self.archive = []\n        self.success_F = []\n        self.success_CR = []\n        self.memory_idx = 0\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\n        self.restart_mask = np.zeros(self.pop_size, dtype=bool)\n\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n        lb = self.lower_bounds\n        ub = self.upper_bounds\n        \n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n\n        # If a known starting point is provided, use it as the first individual\n        if x0 is not None:\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).reshape(-1, self.dim)\n            pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr[0], lb, ub)\n        \n        # Evaluate initial population\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        \n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        # Track best\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        \n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n\n        # Main loop\n        iteration = 0\n        done = False\n        \n        while not done:\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                done = True\n                break\n            iteration += 1\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            \n            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n\n            # Allow the controller to dynamically tune JADE/SHADE hyper-parameters\n            self.p_best = float(params.get('p_best', self.p_best))\n            self.memory_c = float(params.get('memory_c', self.memory_c))\n            self.stagnation_limit = int(params.get('stagnation_limit', self.stagnation_limit))\n            self.restart_radius_factor = float(params.get('restart_radius_factor', self.restart_radius_factor))\n            self.local_search_budget_ratio = float(params.get('local_search_budget_ratio', self.local_search_budget_ratio))\n            \n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n            \n            # ---- JADE-style adaptive mutation / crossover with archive ----\n            new_pop = np.zeros_like(pop)\n            trial_F = np.full(pop_size, F)\n            trial_CR = np.full(pop_size, CR)\n\n            # Sort indices by fitness for pbest selection\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Build combined population + external archive\n            combined_pop = list(pop)\n            combined_fit = list(fitness)\n            if len(self.archive) > 0:\n                combined_pop.extend([ind for ind, _ in self.archive])\n                combined_fit.extend([f for _, f in self.archive])\n            combined_len = len(combined_pop)\n\n            # Random memory index for each individual\n            r_mem = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size, size=pop_size)\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # Per-individual adaptive F and CR\n                if len(self.success_F) > 0 or len(self.success_CR) > 0:\n                    # JADE: F from Cauchy(mu_F, 0.1), CR from Normal(mu_CR, 0.1)\n                    mu_F = float(self.memory_F[r_mem[i]])\n                    mu_CR = float(self.memory_CR[r_mem[i]])\n                    f_i = mu_F + 0.1 * float(rng.standard_cauchy())\n                    cr_i = mu_CR + 0.1 * float(rng.randn())\n                    f_i = min(1.0, max(0.1, f_i))\n                    cr_i = min(1.0, max(0.0, cr_i))\n                else:\n                    f_i = F\n                    cr_i = CR\n                trial_F[i] = f_i\n                trial_CR[i] = cr_i\n\n                # pbest selection: random among top p*100% population (excluding i itself)\n                p_num = max(1, int(self.p_best * pop_size))\n                top_indices = sorted_idx[:p_num]\n                valid_pbest = [idx for idx in top_indices if idx != i]\n                if len(valid_pbest) == 0:\n                    pbest_idx = i\n                else:\n                    pbest_idx = valid_pbest[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_pbest)))]\n\n                # r1 from population, excluding i and pbest\n                pop_candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\n                if len(pop_candidates) == 0:\n                    r1_idx = i\n                else:\n                    r1_idx = pop_candidates[int(rng.randint(0, len(pop_candidates)))]\n                x_r1 = pop[r1_idx]\n\n                # x_r2 from population or archive, excluding i, pbest, r1\n                excluded = {i, pbest_idx, r1_idx}\n                valid_combined = [ci for ci in range(combined_len) if ci not in excluded]\n                if len(valid_combined) == 0:\n                    r2_idx = r1_idx\n                else:\n                    r2_idx = valid_combined[int(rng.randint(0, len(valid_combined)))]\n                if r2_idx < pop_size:\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2_idx]\n                else:\n                    x_r2 = combined_pop[r2_idx]\n\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\n                xi = pop[i]\n\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                mutant = xi + f_i * (x_pbest - xi) + f_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\n\n                # Binomial crossover with ensured at least one dimension changed\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < cr_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, xi)\n\n                # Boundary handling: clip\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and select with external archive\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    done = True\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Archive old individual before replacement\n                    if len(self.archive) < self.archive_capacity:\n                        self.archive.append((pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i])))\n                    else:\n                        replace_idx = int(rng.randint(0, self.archive_capacity))\n                        self.archive[replace_idx] = (pop[i].copy(), float(fitness[i]))\n\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    # Record successful adaptive parameters\n                    self.success_F.append(float(trial_F[i]))\n                    self.success_CR.append(float(trial_CR[i]))\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n\n            # Update JADE/SHADE memory using weighted Lehmer means of successful params\n            if len(self.success_F) > 0:\n                succ_F = np.array(self.success_F, dtype=float)\n                succ_CR = np.array(self.success_CR, dtype=float)\n                if np.sum(succ_F) > 1e-12:\n                    mu_F = float(np.sum(succ_F ** 2) / np.sum(succ_F))\n                else:\n                    mu_F = float(np.mean(succ_F))\n                if len(succ_CR) > 0:\n                    mu_CR = float(np.sum(succ_CR * succ_CR) / np.sum(succ_CR)) if np.sum(succ_CR) > 1e-12 else float(np.mean(succ_CR))\n                else:\n                    mu_CR = float(np.mean(succ_CR)) if len(succ_CR) > 0 else self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx]\n                # SHADE sliding memory update\n                self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] = (1.0 - self.memory_c) * self.memory_F[self.memory_idx] + self.memory_c * mu_F\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] = (1.0 - self.memory_c) * self.memory_CR[self.memory_idx] + self.memory_c * mu_CR\n                self.memory_idx = (self.memory_idx + 1) % self.memory_size\n                self.success_F = []\n                self.success_CR = []\n            \n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n            \n            # Stagnation handling: restart half the population around best\n            if improved > 0.0:\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\n            else:\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\n                if self.stagnation_count >= self.stagnation_limit:\n                    pop, _fitness_ignored = self._restart_population(pop, fitness, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, self.restart_radius_factor)\n                    self.stagnation_count = 0\n            \n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n            \n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n\n        # Final local polish on the best solution using a small portion of remaining budget\n        remaining = self.max_evals - evals\n        if remaining > 0:\n            polish_budget = int(remaining * self.local_search_budget_ratio)\n            if polish_budget > 0:\n                evals, gbest, gbest_f = self._local_polish(problem, evals, gbest, gbest_f, lb, ub, rng, polish_budget)\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n            'evals': int(evals),\n            'iterations': int(iteration),\n            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n        }\n        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\"\n    }\n    </json>",

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file.
